## v56 / 0.56.0, weather dropdown (Sep 2026)
Direct request: the menu bar's one-line weather text ("14° Cloudy") now opens a dropdown, same interaction as the clock's notif panel v42 shipped, reused rather than reinvented. Read `gui_draw_notif_panel` and its whole `gui_run` wiring first, then copied the contract exactly: a `weather_open`/`weather_opening`/`weather_draw_pending` trio mirroring `notif_open`'s own three, open-on-press and dismiss-on-any-following-release, `logo_here`/`clock_here`/`slot_here` all gated with `!weather_open` the same way they already gate `!notif_open`, the dock inert while it's up. `gui_draw_weather_panel` reuses the notif panel's exact chrome, same constants, not restyled: `bg = 0x002C2C2E`, `border = 0x001C1C1E`, `text = 0x00F5F5F7`, same flat-rect-plus-1px-border draw order, same font path (no separate font wiring exists to reuse or diverge from).

The click target itself needed real geometry, not a guess: the weather string's x-position already shifts with both the clock's own width (`p`, 18-20 chars depending on the day/hour's leading zero) and the weather string's own length, so `gui_draw_menubar` now writes the drawn box into two new globals (`weather_hit_x0`/`weather_hit_x1`) every time it actually redraws that text, same minute-granularity the clock's own fixed `mx >= width-200` hit test already tolerates.

Honest about what's real in the panel and what isn't: `weather_fetch` only ever parses `temperature_2m` and `weather_code` out of the Open-Meteo reply today (see the Weather-reactive wallpaper entry above, still not started, for the humidity/wind gap), so the dropdown shows exactly those two, plus the fixed lat/lon literal already sitting in the `http_get` call (not a place name that isn't really in the data) and nothing invented. New state: `weather_temp_c`, `weather_code10`, `weather_have`, set alongside the existing `weather_text` formatting in `weather_fetch` rather than re-parsed.

Verified: `make kernel.elf` clean (only the pre-existing `gui_draw_hello_script` unused-function warning), `./check.sh` -> `PASS: booted, reached gui_run`, `./tools/checks/check-refs.sh` -> clean. Mid-pass course correction, recorded honestly: started toward a live `-display cocoa` window + `cliclick` + `screencapture` per the usual real-hardware standard, but the coordinator flagged that a second visible QEMU window was confusing Joshua watching the real, shared display, so that window was killed unopened-to-completion and this pass verified headless instead, via the same boot-time direct-call trick `check.sh` and the v54 Calendar pass both already use: a temporary block in `kmain` (faked a weather reading since this run had no NIC, drew the desktop with the panel open, sampled real pixels plus the computed hit-box into fixed RAM, `for(;;) hlt`, removed before commit, `git diff` clean) read back under `-display none` via the monitor's `xp`: `0x9010` (a background-only column of the panel's first row) = `0x002c2c2e`, exactly the panel's own `bg` constant; `0x9014` (the panel's top-left border pixel) = `0x001c1c1e`, exactly its own `border` constant, both byte-identical to `gui_draw_notif_panel`'s; `0x9018` (pixels differing from background across a 64x8 strip of the first text row) = `0x64` (100 of 512), real antialiased glyph ink, not a blank rect; `0x901c` (the computed `weather_hit_x0`) = `0x2b8` (696), a sane in-bounds column for a 960-wide logical window, left of the clock as intended. What this does NOT prove, honestly: the actual click landing on a live cursor position, or how it looks next to the real wallpaper/dock in a real screenshot; that's the real gap for a next pass with the display free.

## v59 / 0.58.0, Mail, and the dock/Apps folder reordered to actually mirror stock macOS (Sep 2026)
Direct request: "make sure all the codebase's ported apps are represented and the dock mirrors a stock macOS dock's shape, Finder-equivalent/Mail/Calendar/Notes/Reminders as a recognizable core group, Trash last." Mail was the one real stock-macOS core app this kernel didn't have a local version of yet (Files already stands in for Finder, and Notes/Reminders/Calendar already shipped in v7/v52/v54); `kernel/mail.h` fills that gap, same file-per-app shape reminders.h and calendar.h already established: a fixed `mail_msg_t` array (`from`/`subject`/`body`/`read`), write-through to `MAIL.TXT` on every mutation (`|`-delimited fields, manual scan, no `sscanf`), a list view (up/down, enter reads, `c` composes via the same three-field text-capture loop `reminders_add_new` pioneered, `d` deletes), and a full-screen read view reusing `gui_launch_html`'s own `render_wrapped_text` for the body. Two starter messages are compiled in (`mail_msgs`' own static initializer) so the inbox isn't blank the first time anyone opens it, same call Notes/Reminders made; a real (even empty) `MAIL.TXT` always wins over them after that. One real gotcha worth recording: `mail_msg_t` is large enough (~320 bytes) that a plain `mail_msgs[j] = mail_msgs[j+1]` struct assignment risked clang lowering it to a call to `memcpy`, a libc symbol this freestanding, no-libc build doesn't link (confirmed no `memcpy`/`memset` definition exists anywhere in the tree); the delete path copies each field byte-by-byte instead, the same reason reminders.h's own delete loop never used a bulk copy either. New envelope glyph, `gui_icon_mail`: a rectangle body plus a real V-shaped flap (two `gui_draw_capsule` strokes into the body's own centre), distinct at dock size from Calendar's page-and-rings and Notes' pencil.

Second half, same commit: `GUI_LABELS` (and every switch/if-chain keyed off its indices, all moved together) reordered from "whatever order things got built in" to Files first (Finder), Mail/Calendar/Notes/Reminders as one contiguous core cluster, Terminal/Chat/Weather as the utility group, then every fleet app, Apps and Trash still fixed at the very end (v39's own call, untouched). `GUI_DOCK_DEFAULT` grew from 8 to 10 pinned slots to actually seat that core cluster in the dock itself, not just the Apps-folder grid: Apps, Files, Mail, Calendar, Notes, Reminders, Terminal, Chat, Weather, Trash. Curbfind, the one fleet app previously pinned alongside the utility group, moved to Apps-folder-only, a deliberate call: with four more core apps now competing for pinned slots, one arbitrary fleet app in an otherwise-utility dock no longer read as "the core macOS group," and it's still one click away like every other fleet app. Addressing the standing v37 "make the dock auto size" concern specifically for this change: `gui_dock_icon()` already auto-fits every tile to `DOCK_BUDGET` regardless of `GUI_ICON_COUNT` (confirmed by reading it, not assumed), so growing the dock from 8 to 10 icons needed zero layout changes, no icon shrank past the 16px legibility floor; that half of v37's request was already real, this is just the first change since v37 to actually exercise it past 8 icons. The "intelligently filter only important apps" half stays satisfied the same way it has since v37: every ported app (18 of them now, Mail included) is guaranteed a slot in the Apps folder launchpad (`gui_launch_apps` loops `0..GUI_APPS_FOLDER`, the same array `GUI_LABELS`/`gui_launch` are keyed off, so a new app in that array is automatically in the launchpad with zero extra wiring), while the pinned dock stays a deliberately curated subset, confirmed here by inspection: every embedded HTML app (`app_*_html`) and every native app (Chat/Files/Notes/Terminal/Reminders/Calendar/Mail) has exactly one `GUI_LABELS` slot, no ported app is missing from the launchpad.

Verified for real, not assumed: `make kernel.elf` clean (only the pre-existing `gui_draw_hello_script` unused-function warning), `./check.sh` -> `PASS: booted, reached gui_run`, `./tools/checks/check-refs.sh` -> clean. Headless from the start this pass (direct instruction not to open a live `-display cocoa` window while Joshua is watching the real shared display), via the same boot-time direct-call trick v54/v56/v58 all used: a temporary `window_debug_fb_addr()` accessor in `drivers/window.c` plus a one-line marker write (fb address `0xfd000000`, confirmed via the monitor's `xp /1xw 0x9010`) right after `window_open_scaled`, then a `pmemsave` of the full 1920x1080x32bpp framebuffer under `-display none -vga std`, converted BGRA->PNG in Python and actually inspected the image. Two real dumps: (1) `gui_run()`'s normal boot, cropped to the dock band, shows exactly 10 tiles left to right: 3x3 grid (Apps), blue folder (Files), a real envelope with a visible white V flap (Mail, new), page-with-rings (Calendar), pencil (Notes), green checklist bars (Reminders), dark terminal prompt (Terminal), green chat bubble (Chat), sunburst (Weather), trash bin (Trash), confirming both the new glyph and the exact reordered sequence, not just "it compiled." (2) a second temporary boot path calling `gui_launch_mail()` (then `mail_read_message(0)`) directly, confirming the list view renders both seeded messages ("Joshua Tree, Welcome to Mail", "Joshua Tree, About this app") with the unread marker and selection highlight, and the read view renders from/subject/divider/wrapped body correctly. All debug instrumentation (`window_debug_fb_addr` in `drivers/window.c`/`.h`, the marker writes, the temporary `kmain` detour) fully reverted before commit; `git diff` against the shipped tree touches only `kernel/kernel.c` (reorder + new glyph + dispatch + include) and the new `kernel/mail.h`.
